Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on complex_analysis structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 82. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 715 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_82(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 82. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^715(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a complex_analysis algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 781. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 369/5.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 781 + O(1/n^369). Summing over all modes and applying the complex_analysis structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
